Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any microwave module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 974. The projective resolution has length at most 450 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on microwave structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 974. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 450. QED.
